    7149             : /**

    7151             :  *
    7152             :  * Mirrors what BMesh achieves via #update_uv_vert_map + #bevel_merge_uvs:
    7153             :  *
    7154             :  * 1. **Source corners at original vertices** - average source-corner UV values within each
    7155             :  *    pre-built #UVVertBucket (same as before).
    7156             :  *
    7157             :  * 2. **New corners at original vertices** - should not exist for correctly built bevel geometry
    7158             :  *    (bevel vertices are killed before output), but handled for safety via source-bucket lookup.
    7159             :  *
    7160             :  * 3. **New corners at new (profile-arc) vertices** - look up the arc vertex's parent bevel
    7161             :  *    vertex via #vert_bev_origin, then use that parent's UV bucket structure to group new
    7162             :  *    corners.  New corners whose face representative's source corner at the parent vertex
    7163             :  *    belongs to the same UV bucket get averaged together.  This correctly handles both
    7164             :  *    seam-free meshes (one bucket: all corners merge) and seam meshes (multiple buckets:
    7165             :  *    corners from different UV islands remain separate).
